## D5 physical-wire diagnostics and three additional paths (2026-10-06)

Added test-only `packages/runtime/test/helpers/native-refresh-wire-probe.mjs`. In an isolated fresh worker it temporarily observes the existing Node spawn binding, stdin write/end calls and the original stdout async iterator, restoring the binding in finally. It does not add a relay process, data listener, second reader, whole-stream buffer, source/log dump or product option. Counters/hash update original bytes before forwarding: LF-inclusive totals, physical LF record counts, LF-exclusive maximum line sizes, termination flags and SHA-256. Probe scope is explicitly stdin write-call bytes and stdout bytes delivered to the consumer, not proof of all bytes consumed/emitted by Rust on an interrupted stream. Failed operations preserve their original error and collected counters. UTF8 split writes/whitespace, unterminated failed-child output and binding restoration are covered independently.

- Application/Runtime slice: `applyFullAnalysis` validates source/snapshot and calls the opaque FactStore port exactly once only for `SUCCEEDED`; Runtime composes one staged Native stream, normalizes/merges only a completed success, and drops TEMP state in `finally`. RED: both command/composition tests failed on absent exports. GREEN: direct TypeScript builds of all 16 projects and IR type tests exit 0; `java-basic-symbol` runs through the real Native binary into a migrated temporary database, then verifies persisted Canonical nodes, active Evidence, coverage, and `CURRENT`. The helper cases verify zero-fact success, `PARTIAL` retention, analyzer failure, cancellation, and changed-source `STALE` retention; Store failure-injection cases above verify transactional rollback.
- Independent review found four Important gaps. A valid-checksum v1 success could omit requested files; staged normalization now compares every requested file by indexed TEMP ID lookup and checks the count, without a whole-request JS map or per-fact request scan. Store separately requires the staged inventory to equal the persisted full revision inventory, and rejects dangling coverage/diagnostic source references before destructive writes. Missing-file and dangling-reference regressions failed before these repairs and then `node --test packages/storage/test/canonical-fact-store.test.mjs packages/runtime/test/full-analysis-composition.test.mjs` passed 13/13; focused staged normalization passed 5/5. The fourth gap, absent segmented performance evidence, is addressed by measured Runtime timings, SQLite TEMP size, and Store lock duration below. No threshold or Phase 0.5 exit criterion was relaxed.
- Observed single-file Cold Index baseline, not a Quality Gate: `node --test packages/storage/test/canonical-fact-store.test.mjs packages/runtime/test/full-analysis-composition.test.mjs` on this Windows worktree reported `transportMs=92.9`, `normalizeMs=8.7`, `mergeMs=8.0`, `dbApplyMs=14.2` (including `dbLockMs=10.2`), `totalMs=124.5`, `stageBytes=110592`, and `sampledPeakNodeRssBytes=69828608` for `java-basic-symbol` with one parser worker and a debug Rust binary. TEMP migration and DESCRIBE precede this timing. Transport includes Native scan/parse/resolve, process startup, and IPC; these internal phases and Native-child peak RSS are not separable in this Runtime measurement. Separately, `./scripts/bench-native-symbol-memory.ps1 -FieldCounts 1000` on its deterministic 9,904-byte `unique-fields` Java input reported Native `peakWorkingSetBytes=7536640`, exit 0, `ANALYSIS_COMPLETED`; it is not a peak-memory figure for `java-basic-symbol`. P05-W9-001 retains pinned multi-size, internal-stage, and Worker Consistency telemetry. No performance threshold is introduced or claimed.

- Status: integrated into `main` by PR #10 at `a9180d8` after local acceptance and independent review. The review found five Important gaps, which received local repairs and fresh gates on 2026-09-25; read-only re-review found no remaining Important code blocker under the explicit provisional memory-policy limitation. Java Tree-sitter `0.23.5`, Tree-sitter `0.27.0`, SHA-2 `0.10.9`, and cap-std `4.0.3` are pinned in Cargo. No Frozen schema, migration, or numerical Quality Gate changed.
- Fixture and quality evidence: the registry discovers `java-basic-symbol` and `java-nested-types` at `fixtures/native/*/fixture.json`. `packages/providers/test/native-analysis.test.mjs` runs the real Rust binary through the production TypeScript Native client and compares every ProviderNode, coverage record, and diagnostic against checked-in goldens. All 9 declared critical Java SYMBOL facts match by source-grounded path/kind/qualified name/source-spelled signature (100% recall versus the existing at-least-95% gate). The adapter has no Spring/framework fact path; malformed Java yields explicit `PARTIAL`, and empty Java yields successful zero-fact output. `DESCRIBE` now advertises evaluated `SYMBOL` with pinned Java grammar version while preserving protocol/IR v1 and the existing handshake behavior.
- Security, determinism, recovery: the Native reader validates absolute root, canonical relative path, every ancestor/leaf no-link/reparse status, a capability-relative open, bounded bytes, before/after metadata, and raw-byte SHA-256. Five Rust source-access tests pass, including Windows junction and symlink redirection, same-length replacement, and budget failure. `ANALYZE` uses exact request context, ordered events, contiguous sequence, terminal counts, and raw JSONL checksum. Worker count is resolved from the sole runtime-local `parseWorkers` mode and passed as a private argument; Rust rechecks the memory budget. A two-worker late-completion/slow-drain test observes completed-result high-water 2 and source-order output. Client failure tests reject stale source, wrong checksum, count drift, truncated output, timeout, and cancellation without exposing applicable partial results.
- Initial resource policy evidence (superseded after review): on 2026-09-25, five Windows x86-64 MSVC debug runs each of the pinned basic/nested sources sampled child `PeakWorkingSet64` while asynchronously draining both pipes. Basic peaks were 5.51/2.98/2.55/2.62/2.55 MiB; nested peaks were 2.14/2.80/2.98/2.98/2.98 MiB. The initial 16 MiB accounted worker reservation and 8 MiB input cap were insufficiently justified for dense syntax; the reproducible stress evidence and narrower current policy are recorded below. At most one completed batch of `parseWorkers` results is retained. This is not an OS hard limit or a new performance gate; later Phase 0.5 Evaluation must test and adjust it from pinned evidence.
- Pre-review local gates (superseded by the post-review evidence below): direct Prettier, 16 TypeScript builds and IR type tests, the 17-project architecture check, root-script Node fileset (528/529 with one Windows symlink-privilege skip), real-binary Native suites (8/8), Rust format/build/Clippy/tests, and six existing Evaluation gates passed. `pnpm verify` exited before scripts with `ERR_PNPM_VERIFY_DEPS_BEFORE_RUN` because the desktop worktree's installed modules do not match its lockfile. Post-review direct gates and hosted clean-install evidence are recorded below.
- Limitations and next task: P05-W3-001 does not extract imports/calls/inheritance/references (P05-W3-002), complete the outcome matrix (P05-W3-003), implement REFRESH/stale graph removal, normalize or activate Canonical Facts, write SQLite, publish Knowledge, or implement framework/Wiki/Diagram/Agent features. The current small-Fixture resource measurements do not establish large-repository behavior. Next dependency-valid task after review/integration is P05-W3-002; Phase 0.5 promotion still requires every separate exit gate.
- Post-review repair and fresh evidence (2026-09-25): the Rust coordinator uses timed batch receive and parser progress cancellation; an injected slow-worker test proves prompt deadline failure. BINARY SourceFiles are rejected before reading. Varargs and post-declarator dimensions enter signature identity, and overload IDs survive declaration reorder. IPC line/total-byte overflow ends in `ANALYSIS_FAILED`. A 9,000-field Java source exercises the 4 MiB estimated result ceiling. On implementation commit `7247266998f3ed8fcc79ba7659f955697257e8a5`, `cargo test --workspace -j 1` passed 8 Rust unit, 8 ANALYZE integration, 1 handshake, 7 Java adapter, 4 protocol-Fixture, 5 smoke, and 5 source-access tests; an earlier transient Windows `LNK1104` cleared on unchanged retry. `cargo build --workspace -j 1` and the production-client real-binary Node suites passed 8/8; `node scripts/ekp-dev.mjs eval fixtures/evaluation/metrics-v1` returned six PASS gates. The root-script Node fileset passed 528/529 with one pre-existing Windows leaf-symlink privilege skip; installed `tsc` built all 16 projects and IR type tests; the 17-project architecture check, direct Prettier, Rust format/Clippy, and Git whitespace checks passed. Dense-source `PeakWorkingSet64` samples from `scripts/bench-native-symbol-memory.ps1` exposed the initial 8 MiB-cap defect: 97,904 source bytes / 9,000 unique fields peaked at 17,932,288 working-set bytes despite the result guard. The provisional 64 KiB pre-parse admission cap rejects the same over-budget sample before parsing; one post-cap run peaked at 4,325,376 bytes. A 64,904-byte unique-fields input completed at 13,860,864 peak bytes, and an admitted 63,014-byte short-fields input reached its retained-result limit at 14,708,736 peak bytes with a typed failed terminal. This is not an allocator-enforced RSS ceiling or a cross-platform guarantee. Independent read-only re-review found no remaining Important code blocker under that limitation. `pnpm verify` remains blocked before scripts by the worktree dependency mismatch noted above.

The run completed `success`; TypeScript workspace, Native Java analysis integration (including clean-install real-binary Fixtures), and Rust workspace jobs each completed `success`. The approved design and plan commits are `f694bc6` and `08b770c`; implementation commits are `9d8e052`, `5150ed2`, and `f788c2d`. No merge, push, hosted CI, or exact remote-ref equality is claimed.

No Frozen decision, phase definition, numeric acceptance threshold, Canonical IR schema, SQLite schema, or migration changed.
- Implementation and Fixture: the no-argument Rust binary now processes exactly one bounded UTF-8 JSONL `DESCRIBE` request, performs strict envelope/payload validation and protocol-before-IR negotiation, and emits one language-neutral descriptor or terminal `REQUEST_FAILED`. `--smoke` remains explicit; other arguments fail without protocol stdout. `p0-native-ipc-handshake-v1` at `fixtures/ipc/handshake-v1/cases.json` declares five byte-exact compatible, incompatible-protocol, incompatible-IR, invalid-request, and malformed-request outcomes. `crates/native-analyzer/tests/handshake.rs` and test-only `packages/providers/test/native-handshake.test.mjs` execute the real binary; the TypeScript verifier applies the existing request, event, and complete-stream schemas with direct shell-free spawning, bounded capture, and a five-second timeout.

Output-writer failure propagates to the process boundary. No state exists to partially apply. - Implementation: `@codeatlas/providers` now exports `executeNativeRequest`, an immutable closed error model, and the production client configuration/result types. - Integrity and environment boundaries: successful `ANALYZE` and `REFRESH` results verify lowercase SHA-256 over the exact raw pre-terminal JSONL bytes including LF before exposure. The caller environment allowlist is `LANG`, `LC_ALL`, `NO_COLOR`, `SYSTEMROOT`, `WINDIR`, `TEMP`, and `TMP`. Windows parent variables are blanked before allowlist overlay because Node can otherwise restore host values; the Windows-synthesized `NUMBER_OF_PROCESSORS` is documented as platform-intrinsic and must not be treated as trusted configuration. Bounded stderr is available only as explicit diagnostic metadata on typed failures and is omitted from default error serialization.
